\# twitterapi.io ## Docs - \[Introduction\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/introduction.md): twitterapi.io docs.The best third-party Twitter API: reliable, high-performance, supports high QPS, and cost-effective. - \[Authentication\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/authentication.md): Learn how to authenticate your API requests using API keys - \[Batch Get User Info By UserIds\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/batch\_get\_user\_by\_userids.md): Batch get user info by user ids. Pricing: - \[Get User Info\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get\_user\_by\_username.md): Get user info by screen name - \[Get User TimeLine\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get\_user\_timeline.md): Retrieve tweets by user id.Sort by created\_at. Results are paginated, with each page returning up to 20 tweets.The content you see is in the same order as the tweets on the user's profile in the Twitter app, and filtering by time is not supported. - \[Get User Last Tweets\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get\_user\_last\_tweets.md): Retrieve tweets by user name.Sort by created\_at. Results are paginated, with each page returning up to 20 tweets.If you only need to fetch the latest tweets from a single user very frequently, do not use this API—it will cost you a lot. Instead, please refer to https://twitterapi.io/blog/how-to-moni… - \[Get User Followers\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get\_user\_followers.md): Get user followers (with full profile metadata) in reverse chronological order (newest first). Sorted by follow date — most recent followers appear on the first page. Use \`cursor\` for pagination through the complete followers list. - \[Get User Followers IDs (Bulk)\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get\_user\_followers\_ids.md): Get a user's follower IDs in bulk — \*\*lightweight, IDs only, no profile metadata\*\*. Designed for large-scale follower-graph collection where you join IDs against your own data warehouse. - \[Get User Followings\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get\_user\_followings.md): Get user followings (with full profile metadata). Sorted by follow date — most recent followings appear on the first page. Use \`cursor\` for pagination. - \[Get User Mentions\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get\_user\_mention.md): get tweet mentions by user screen name.Each page returns exactly 20 mentions. Use cursor for pagination. Order by mention time desc - \[Check Follow Relationship\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/check\_follow\_relationship.md): Check if the user is following/followed by the target user. Trial operation price: 100 credits per call. - \[Search user by keyword\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/search\_user.md): Search user by keyword - \[Get User Verified Followers\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get\_user\_verified\_followers.md): Get user verified followers in reverse chronological order (newest first). Returns exactly 20 verified followers per page, sorted by follow date. Most recent followers appear on the first page. Use cursor for pagination through the complete followers list.$0.3 per 1000 followers - \[Get User Profile About\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get\_user\_about.md): Get user profile about by screen name - \[Get Tweets by IDs\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get\_tweet\_by\_ids.md): get tweet by tweet ids - \[Get Tweet Replies\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get\_tweet\_reply.md): get tweet replies by tweet id.Each page returns up to 20 replies(Sometimes less than 20,because we will filter out ads or other not tweets). Use cursor for pagination. Order by reply time desc - \[Get Tweet Replies V2\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get\_tweet\_replies\_v2.md): Get tweet replies by tweet id (V2). Each page returns up to 20 replies. Use cursor for pagination. Supports sorting by Relevance, Latest, or Likes. - \[Get Tweet Quotations\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get\_tweet\_quote.md): get tweet quotes by tweet id.Each page returns exactly 20 quotes. Use cursor for pagination. Order by quote time desc - \[Get Tweet Retweeters\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get\_tweet\_retweeter.md): get tweet retweeters by tweet id.Each page returns about 100 retweeters. Use cursor for pagination. Order by retweet time desc - \[Get Tweet Thread Context\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get\_tweet\_thread\_context.md): Get the thread context of a tweet.Suppose a tweet thread consists of t1, t2 (replying to t1), t3 (replying to t2), and t4, t5, t6 (all replying to t3). Use cursor for pagination. - \[Get List Followers\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get\_list\_followers.md): Get followers of a list. Page size is 20. - \[Get List Members\](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get\_list\_members.md): Get members of a list. cURL

cURL

    curl --request POST \
      --url https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/x_user_stream/add_user_to_monitor_tweet \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --header 'X-API-Key: <api-key>' \
      --data '
    {
      "x_user_name": "<string>"
    }
    '

    import requests
    
    url = "https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/x_user_stream/add_user_to_monitor_tweet"
    
    payload = { "x_user_name": "<string>" }
    headers = {
        "X-API-Key": "<api-key>",
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
    }
    
    response = requests.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {'X-API-Key': '<api-key>', 'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({x_user_name: '<string>'})
    };
    
    fetch('https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/x_user_stream/add_user_to_monitor_tweet', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    <?php
    
    $curl = curl_init();
    
    curl_setopt_array($curl, [\
      CURLOPT_URL => "https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/x_user_stream/add_user_to_monitor_tweet",\
      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "POST",\

        'x_user_name' => '<string>'\
      ]),\
      CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => [\
        "Content-Type: application/json",\
        "X-API-Key: <api-key>"\

    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);
    
    if ($err) {
      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;

    package main
    
    import (
    	"fmt"
    	"strings"
    	"net/http"
    	"io"
    )
    
    func main() {
    
    	url := "https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/x_user_stream/add_user_to_monitor_tweet"
    
    	payload := strings.NewReader("{\n  \"x_user_name\": \"<string>\"\n}")
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", url, payload)
    
    	req.Header.Add("X-API-Key", "<api-key>")
    	req.Header.Add("Content-Type", "application/json")
    
    	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
    
    	defer res.Body.Close()
    	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
    
    	fmt.Println(string(body))
    
    }

    HttpResponse<String> response = Unirest.post("https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/x_user_stream/add_user_to_monitor_tweet")
      .header("X-API-Key", "<api-key>")
      .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
      .body("{\n  \"x_user_name\": \"<string>\"\n}")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'
    require 'net/http'
    
    url = URI("https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/x_user_stream/add_user_to_monitor_tweet")
    
    http = Net::HTTP.new(url.host, url.port)
    http.use_ssl = true
    
    request = Net::HTTP::Post.new(url)
    request["X-API-Key"] = '<api-key>'
    request["Content-Type"] = 'application/json'
    request.body = "{\n  \"x_user_name\": \"<string>\"\n}"
    
    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/bookmark_tweet_v2#body-tweet-id)

tweet\_id

string

required

The id of the tweet to bookmark. Must be set

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/bookmark_tweet_v2#body-proxy)

proxy

string

required

The proxy to use. Please use high-quality residential proxies and avoid free proxies. Required. Example: http://username:password@ip:port \[blocked\]

#### Response

200

application/json

Bookmark response

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/bookmark_tweet_v2#response-status)

status

string

Status of the request. success or error

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/bookmark_tweet_v2#response-message)

message

string

Message of the request. error message

#### Authorizations

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/create_tweet_v2#authorization-x-api-key)

X-API-Key

string

header

required

#### Body

application/json

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/create_tweet_v2#body-login-cookies)

login\_cookies

string

required

The login cookies of the user.You can get the login\_cookie from /twitter/user\_login\_v2.Must be set

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/create_tweet_v2#body-tweet-text)

tweet\_text

string

required

The text of the tweet.Must be set

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/create_tweet_v2#body-proxy)

proxy

string

required

The proxy to use.Please use high-quality residential proxies and avoid free proxies.Required.Example: http://username:password@ip:port \[blocked\] . You can get proxy from: [https://www.webshare.io/?referral\_code=4e0q1n00a504](https://www.webshare.io/?referral_code=4e0q1n00a504)

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/create_tweet_v2#body-reply-to-tweet-id)

reply\_to\_tweet\_id

string

The id of the tweet to reply to.Optional

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/create_tweet_v2#body-attachment-url)

attachment\_url

string

The url of quote tweet.Optional.eg.[https://x.com/sama/status/1947640330318156074](https://x.com/sama/status/1947640330318156074)

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/create_tweet_v2#body-community-id)

community\_id

string

The id of the community to post to.Optional

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/create_tweet_v2#body-is-note-tweet)

is\_note\_tweet

boolean

If this option is set to True, tweets longer than 280 characters can be posted (Twitter Premium only).

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/create_tweet_v2#body-media-ids)

media\_ids

string\[\]

List of media IDs (strings) to attach. Optional. Obtain each from /twitter/upload\_media\_v2.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/create_tweet_v2#body-quote-tweet-id)

quote\_tweet\_id

string

ID of a tweet to quote. Optional. Alternative to attachment\_url.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/create_tweet_v2#body-schedule-for)

schedule\_for

string

Schedule the tweet to be sent at a future time. Optional. Format: ISO-8601 with milliseconds, e.g. 2026-01-20T10:00:00.000Z

#### Response

200

application/json

Login response

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/create_tweet_v2#response-tweet-id)

tweet\_id

string

The id of the created tweet.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/create_tweet_v2#response-status)

status

string

Status of the request.success or error

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/create_tweet_v2#response-msg)

msg

string

Message of the request.error message

        "proxy": "<string>",

#### Authorizations

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/bookmarks_v2#authorization-x-api-key)

X-API-Key

string

header

required

#### Body

application/json

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/bookmarks_v2#body-login-cookies)

login\_cookies

string

required

The login cookies of the user. You can get the login\_cookie from /twitter/user\_login\_v2. Must be set

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/bookmarks_v2#body-proxy)

proxy

string

required

The proxy to use. Please use high-quality residential proxies and avoid free proxies. Required. Example: http://username:password@ip:port \[blocked\]

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/bookmarks_v2#body-count)

count

integer

default:20

Number of bookmarks to return per page. Default is 20

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/bookmarks_v2#body-cursor)

cursor

string

Cursor for pagination. Use next\_cursor from previous response to get next page

#### Response

200

application/json

Bookmarks response

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/bookmarks_v2#response-tweets)

tweets

object\[\]

required

Array of bookmarked tweets

Show child attributes

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/bookmarks_v2#response-has-next-page)

has\_next\_page

boolean

required

Indicates if there are more results available

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/bookmarks_v2#response-next-cursor)

next\_cursor

string

required

Cursor for fetching the next page of results

# Authentication - twitterapi.io

> Documentation Index
> -------------------
> 
> Fetch the complete documentation index at: [/llms.txt](https://docs.twitterapi.io/llms.txt)
> 
> Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.

[Skip to main content](https://docs.twitterapi.io/authentication#content-area)

**Skip writing auth + pagination boilerplate** — install the [official AI agent skill](https://github.com/kaitoInfra/twitterapi-io)
 and let Claude Code, Cursor, Copilot, etc. handle it:

    npx skills add kaitoInfra/twitterapi-io
    

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/authentication#api-authentication)

API Authentication
---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Every API request requires authentication using an API key in the HTTP headers.

### 

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/authentication#getting-your-api-key)

Getting Your API Key

1.  Log in to your [TwitterApiIO Dashboard](https://twitterapi.io/dashboard)
    
2.  Find your API key displayed on the dashboard homepage

### 

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/authentication#using-your-api-key)

Using Your API Key

All API requests must include the `x-api-key` header for authentication. Header format:

    x-api-key: YOUR_API_KEY
    

Example requests:

#### 

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/authentication#curl)

cURL

    curl --location 'https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/user/followings?userName=KaitoEasyAPI' \
    --header 'x-api-key: my_test_xxxxx'
    

#### 

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/authentication#python)

Python

    import requests
    
    url = 'https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/user/followings?userName=KaitoEasyAPI'
    headers = {'x-api-key': 'my_test_xxxxx'}
    response = requests.get(url, headers=headers)
    print(response.json())
    

#### 

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/authentication#java)

Java

    import okhttp3.OkHttpClient;
    import okhttp3.Request;
    import okhttp3.Response;
    
    OkHttpClient client = new OkHttpClient();
    
    Request request = new Request.Builder()
        .url("https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/user/followings?userName=KaitoEasyAPI")
        .addHeader("x-api-key", "my_test_xxxxx")
        .build();
    
    try (Response response = client.newCall(request).execute()) {
        System.out.println(response.body().string());
    } catch (Exception e) {
        e.printStackTrace();
    }

# Remove a user from  monitor list - twitterapi.io

> Documentation Index
> -------------------
> 
> Fetch the complete documentation index at: [/llms.txt](https://docs.twitterapi.io/llms.txt)
> 
> Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.

[Skip to main content](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/remove_user_to_monitor_tweet#content-area)

cURL

cURL

    curl --request POST \
      --url https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/x_user_stream/remove_user_to_monitor_tweet \
      --header 'Content-Type: application/json' \
      --header 'X-API-Key: <api-key>' \
      --data '
    {
      "id_for_user": "<string>"
    }
    '

    import requests
    
    url = "https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/x_user_stream/remove_user_to_monitor_tweet"
    
    payload = { "id_for_user": "<string>" }
    headers = {
        "X-API-Key": "<api-key>",
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
    }
    
    response = requests.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {'X-API-Key': '<api-key>', 'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({id_for_user: '<string>'})
    };
    
    fetch('https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/x_user_stream/remove_user_to_monitor_tweet', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    <?php
    
    $curl = curl_init();
    
    curl_setopt_array($curl, [\
      CURLOPT_URL => "https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/x_user_stream/remove_user_to_monitor_tweet",\
      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "POST",\
      CURLOPT_POSTFIELDS => json_encode([\
        'id_for_user' => '<string>'\
      ]),\
      CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => [\
        "Content-Type: application/json",\
        "X-API-Key: <api-key>"\
      ],\
    ]);
    
    $response = curl_exec($curl);
    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);
    
    if ($err) {
      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
    } else {
      echo $response;
    }

    package main

    	"fmt"
    	"strings"
    	"net/http"
    	"io"
    )
    
    func main() {
    
    	url := "https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/x_user_stream/remove_user_to_monitor_tweet"
    
    	payload := strings.NewReader("{\n  \"id_for_user\": \"<string>\"\n}")
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("POST", url, payload)
    
    	req.Header.Add("X-API-Key", "<api-key>")
    	req.Header.Add("Content-Type", "application/json")
    
    	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
    
    	defer res.Body.Close()
    	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
    
    	fmt.Println(string(body))
    
    }

    HttpResponse<String> response = Unirest.post("https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/x_user_stream/remove_user_to_monitor_tweet")
      .header("X-API-Key", "<api-key>")
      .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
      .body("{\n  \"id_for_user\": \"<string>\"\n}")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'
    require 'net/http'

    request["X-API-Key"] = '<api-key>'
    request["Content-Type"] = 'application/json'
    request.body = "{\n  \"id_for_user\": \"<string>\"\n}"
    
    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

> Documentation Index

> Fetch the complete documentation index at: [/llms.txt](https://docs.twitterapi.io/llms.txt)
> 
> Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.

[Skip to main content](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_banner_v2#content-area)

cURL

cURL

    curl --request PATCH \
      --url https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/update_banner_v2 \
      --header 'Content-Type: multipart/form-data' \
      --header 'X-API-Key: <api-key>' \
      --form file='@example-file' \
      --form 'proxy=<string>' \
      --form 'login_cookies=<string>'

    import requests
    
    url = "https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/update_banner_v2"
    
    files = { "file": ("example-file", open("example-file", "rb")) }

#### Authorizations

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_banner_v2#authorization-x-api-key)

X-API-Key

string

header

required

#### Body

multipart/form-data

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_banner_v2#body-file)

file

file

required

The banner image file to upload. Recommended size: 1500x500 pixels. Maximum file size: 2MB. Supported formats: JPG, PNG

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_banner_v2#body-proxy)

proxy

string

required

The proxy to use. Please use high-quality residential proxies and avoid free proxies. Required. Example: http://username:password@ip:port \[blocked\]

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_banner_v2#body-login-cookies)

login\_cookies

string

required

The login cookies of the user. You can get the login\_cookie from /twitter/user\_login\_v2. Required

#### Response

200

application/json

Update banner response

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_banner_v2#response-status)

status

string

Status of the request. success or error

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_banner_v2#response-message)

message

string

Message of the request. e.g. Banner updated successfully

string

header

required

application/json

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_webhook_rule#body-rule-id)

rule\_id

string

required

ID of the rule to update.Must be set

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_webhook_rule#body-tag)

tag

string

required

Custom tag to identify the rule.Must be set.Max length is 255

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_webhook_rule#body-value)

value

string

required

Rule to filter tweets.eg "from:elonmusk OR from:kaitoeasyapi".Must be set.Max length is 255

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_webhook_rule#body-interval-seconds)

interval\_seconds

number

required

Interval to check tweets.Must be set.Min value is 0.1.Max value is 86400 ()

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_webhook_rule#body-is-effect)

is\_effect

enum<integer>

Whether the rule is effective.Must be set.1 means effective, 0 means not effective.Default value is 0

Available options:

`0`,

`1`

#### Response

200

application/json

Tweet filter rule update response

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_webhook_rule#response-status)

status

enum<string>

required

Status of the request.success or error

Available options:

`success`,

`error`

msg

string

required

user\_id

string

required

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/unfollow_user_v2#body-proxy)

proxy

string

required

The proxy to use.Please use high-quality residential proxies and avoid free proxies.Required.Example: http://username:password@ip:port \[blocked\] . You can get proxy from: [https://www.webshare.io/?referral\_code=4e0q1n00a504](https://www.webshare.io/?referral_code=4e0q1n00a504)

      --form file='@example-file' \
      --form 'proxy=<string>' \
      --form 'login_cookies=<string>'

    url = "https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/update_avatar_v2"
    
    files = { "file": ("example-file", open("example-file", "rb")) }
    payload = {
        "proxy": "<string>",
        "login_cookies": "<string>"
    }
    headers = {"X-API-Key": "<api-key>"}
    
    response = requests.patch(url, data=payload, files=files, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

    const form = new FormData();
    form.append('file', '<string>');
    form.append('proxy', '<string>');
    form.append('login_cookies', '<string>');
    
    const options = {method: 'PATCH', headers: {'X-API-Key': '<api-key>'}};
    
    options.body = form;
    
    fetch('https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/update_avatar_v2', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    	payload := strings.NewReader("-----011000010111000001101001\r\nContent-Disposition: form-data; name=\"file\"; filename=\"example-file\"\r\nContent-Type: application/octet-stream\r\n\r\n<string>\r\n-----011000010111000001101001\r\nContent-Disposition: form-data; name=\"proxy\"\r\n\r\n<string>\r\n-----011000010111000001101001\r\nContent-Disposition: form-data; name=\"login_cookies\"\r\n\r\n<string>\r\n-----011000010111000001101001--")
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("PATCH", url, payload)

#### Authorizations

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_avatar_v2#authorization-x-api-key)

X-API-Key

string

header

required

#### Body

multipart/form-data

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_avatar_v2#body-file)

file

file

required

The avatar image file to upload. Recommended size: 400x400 pixels. Maximum file size: 700KB. Supported formats: JPG, PNG

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_avatar_v2#body-proxy)

proxy

string

required

The proxy to use. Please use high-quality residential proxies and avoid free proxies. Required. Example: http://username:password@ip:port \[blocked\]

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_avatar_v2#body-login-cookies)

login\_cookies

string

required

The login cookies of the user. You can get the login\_cookie from /twitter/user\_login\_v2. Required

#### Response

200

application/json

Update avatar response

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_avatar_v2#response-status)

status

string

Status of the request. success or error

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_avatar_v2#response-message)

message

string

Message of the request. e.g. Avatar updated successfully

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/user_login_v2#authorization-x-api-key)

X-API-Key

string

header

required

#### Body

application/json

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/user_login_v2#body-user-name)

user\_name

string

required

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/user_login_v2#body-email)

email

string

required

Email.Must be set

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/user_login_v2#body-password)

password

string

required

The password of the user

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/user_login_v2#body-proxy)

proxy

string

required

The proxy to use.Please use high-quality residential proxies and avoid free proxies.Required.Example: http://username:password@ip:port \[blocked\] . You can get proxy from: [https://www.webshare.io/?referral\_code=4e0q1n00a504](https://www.webshare.io/?referral_code=4e0q1n00a504)

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/user_login_v2#body-totp-secret)

totp\_secret

string

TOTP/2FA secret (base32 seed, not a 6-digit code). Optional, but strongly recommended — accounts without 2FA may yield a login\_cookie that gets flagged and can't post. To obtain: on X, enable 2FA, choose "can't scan the QR code", and X shows a 10+ character string — that's the TOTP secret.

#### Response

200

application/json

Login response

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/user_login_v2#response-login-cookie)

login\_cookie

string

The login cookie of the user.Use this cookie to post tweets, etc.You can only call v2 APIs with this cookie.If your account is in good standing and you're using residential proxies, the cookies will generally remain valid indefinitely.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/user_login_v2#response-status)

status

string

Status of the request.success or error

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/user_login_v2#response-msg)

msg

string

Message of the request.error message

      --url https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/my/info \
      --header 'X-API-Key: <api-key>'

    import requests
    
    url = "https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/my/info"
    
    headers = {"X-API-Key": "<api-key>"}
    
    response = requests.get(url, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {method: 'GET', headers: {'X-API-Key': '<api-key>'}};
    
    fetch('https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/my/info', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    <?php
    
    $curl = curl_init();
    
    curl_setopt_array($curl, [\
      CURLOPT_URL => "https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/my/info",\
      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "GET",\
      CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => [\
        "X-API-Key: <api-key>"\
      ],\
    ]);
    
    $response = curl_exec($curl);
    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);
    
    if ($err) {
      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
    } else {
      echo $response;
    }

    package main
    
    import (
    	"fmt"
    	"net/http"
    	"io"
    )
    
    func main() {
    
    	url := "https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/my/info"
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("GET", url, nil)
    
    	req.Header.Add("X-API-Key", "<api-key>")
    
    	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
    
    	defer res.Body.Close()
    	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
    
    	fmt.Println(string(body))
    
    }

    HttpResponse<String> response = Unirest.get("https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/my/info")
      .header("X-API-Key", "<api-key>")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'
    require 'net/http'
    
    url = URI("https://api.twitterapi.io/oapi/my/info")
    
    http = Net::HTTP.new(url.host, url.port)
    http.use_ssl = true
    
    request = Net::HTTP::Get.new(url)
    request["X-API-Key"] = '<api-key>'
    
    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

userName

string<string>

required

screen name of the user

cursor

string<string>

The cursor to paginate through the results. First page is "".

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_followers#parameter-page-size)

pageSize

integer

default:200

The number of followings to return per page. Default is 200.min 20,max 200

#### Authorizations

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_reply#authorization-x-api-key)

X-API-Key

string

header

required

#### Query Parameters

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_reply#parameter-tweet-id)

tweetId

string<string>

required

The tweet ID to get. Must be an original tweet (not a reply to another tweet) and should be the first tweet in a thread.. eg. 1846987139428634858

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_reply#parameter-since-time)

sinceTime

integer<int64>

On or after a specified unix timestamp in seconds.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_reply#parameter-until-time)

untilTime

integer<int64>

Before a specified unix timestamp in seconds.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_reply#parameter-cursor)

cursor

string<string>

The cursor to paginate through the results. First page is "".

#### Response

200

application/json

Tweet replies response

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_reply#response-replies)

replies

object\[\]

Array of tweets

Show child attributes

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_reply#response-has-next-page)

has\_next\_page

boolean

Indicates if there are more results available. If true, use next\_cursor to fetch the next page. Warning: Due to Twitter API inconsistency, has\_more might return true even when no additional data exists. In such cases, subsequent requests will return empty results - this is a known platform limitation.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_reply#response-next-cursor)

next\_cursor

string

Cursor for fetching the next page of results

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_reply#response-status)

status

enum<string>

Status of the request.success or error

Available options:

`success`,

`error`

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_reply#response-message)

message

string

Message of the request.error message

#### Authorizations

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_last_tweets#authorization-x-api-key)

X-API-Key

string

header

required

#### Query Parameters

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_last_tweets#parameter-user-id)

userId

string<string>

user id of the user.userId is recommended, could be more stable and faster than userName.userId and userName are mutually exclusive. If both are provided, userId will be used.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_last_tweets#parameter-user-name)

userName

string<string>

screen name of the user.userId and userName are mutually exclusive. If both are provided, userId will be used.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_last_tweets#parameter-cursor)

cursor

string<string>

The cursor to paginate through the results. First page is "".

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_last_tweets#parameter-include-replies)

includeReplies

boolean

default:false

Whether to include replies in the results. Default is False.

#### Response

200

application/json

Tweets response

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_last_tweets#response-tweets)

tweets

object\[\]

required

Array of tweets

Show child attributes

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_last_tweets#response-has-next-page)

has\_next\_page

boolean

required

Indicates if there are more results available

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_last_tweets#response-next-cursor)

next\_cursor

string

required

Cursor for fetching the next page of results

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_last_tweets#response-status)

status

enum<string>

required

Status of the request.success or error

Available options:

`success`,

`error`

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_last_tweets#response-message)

message

string

required

#### Authorizations

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_profile_v2#authorization-x-api-key)

X-API-Key

string

header

required

#### Body

application/json

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_profile_v2#body-login-cookies)

login\_cookies

string

required

Base64 encoded login cookies of the user. You can get the login\_cookie from /twitter/user\_login\_v2. Required

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_profile_v2#body-proxy)

proxy

string

required

The proxy to use. Please use high-quality residential proxies and avoid free proxies. Required. Example: http://username:password@ip:port \[blocked\]

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_profile_v2#body-name)

name

string

Display name (maximum 50 characters)

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_profile_v2#body-description)

description

string

Bio/description (maximum 160 characters)

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_profile_v2#body-location)

location

string

Location (maximum 30 characters)

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_profile_v2#body-url)

url

string

Website URL

#### Response

200

application/json

Update profile response

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_profile_v2#response-status)

status

string

Status of the request. success or error

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/update_profile_v2#response-message)

message

string

Message of the request. e.g. Profile updated successfully

      --form file='@example-file' \
      --form 'proxy=<string>' \
      --form 'login_cookies=<string>' \
      --form is_long_video=true

    import requests
    
    url = "https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/upload_media_v2"
    
    files = { "file": ("example-file", open("example-file", "rb")) }
    payload = {
        "proxy": "<string>",
        "login_cookies": "<string>",
        "is_long_video": "true"
    }
    headers = {"X-API-Key": "<api-key>"}
    
    response = requests.post(url, data=payload, files=files, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

    const form = new FormData();
    form.append('file', '<string>');
    form.append('proxy', '<string>');
    form.append('login_cookies', '<string>');
    form.append('is_long_video', 'true');
    
    const options = {method: 'POST', headers: {'X-API-Key': '<api-key>'}};
    
    options.body = form;
    
    fetch('https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/upload_media_v2', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

#### Authorizations

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/upload_media_v2#authorization-x-api-key)

X-API-Key

string

header

required

#### Body

multipart/form-data

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/upload_media_v2#body-file)

file

file

required

The media file to upload (image, video, etc.)

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/upload_media_v2#body-proxy)

proxy

string

required

The proxy to use.Please use high-quality residential proxies and avoid free proxies. Required. Example: http://username:password@ip:port \[blocked\] . You can get proxy from: [https://www.webshare.io/?referral\_code=4e0q1n00a504](https://www.webshare.io/?referral_code=4e0q1n00a504)

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/upload_media_v2#body-login-cookies)

login\_cookies

string

required

The login cookies of the user. You can get the login\_cookie from /twitter/user\_login\_v2. Optional for media upload, required for posting tweets

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/upload_media_v2#body-is-long-video)

is\_long\_video

boolean

If this is True, videos longer than 2:20 can be uploaded.(Twitter Premium only)

#### Response

200

application/json

Upload media response

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/upload_media_v2#response-media-id)

media\_id

string

The id of the uploaded media.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/upload_media_v2#response-status)

status

string

Status of the request.success or error

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/upload_media_v2#response-msg)

msg

string

Message of the request.error message

      "user_id": "<string>",
      "text": "<string>",
      "proxy": "<string>",
      "media_id": "<string>",
      "reply_to_message_id": "<string>"

    import requests
    
    url = "https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/send_dm_to_user"
    
    payload = {
        "login_cookies": "<string>",
        "user_id": "<string>",
        "text": "<string>",
        "proxy": "<string>",
        "media_id": "<string>",
        "reply_to_message_id": "<string>"
    }
    headers = {
        "X-API-Key": "<api-key>",
        "Content-Type": "application/json"
    }
    
    response = requests.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

      method: 'POST',
      headers: {'X-API-Key': '<api-key>', 'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
      body: JSON.stringify({
        login_cookies: '<string>',
        user_id: '<string>',
        text: '<string>',
        proxy: '<string>',
        media_id: '<string>',
        reply_to_message_id: '<string>'
      })
    };
    
    fetch('https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/send_dm_to_user', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

proxy

string

required

string

reply\_to\_message\_id

#### Authorizations

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_followers_ids#authorization-x-api-key)

X-API-Key

string

header

required

#### Query Parameters

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_followers_ids#parameter-user-name)

userName

string<string>

Screen name of the user (e.g. `elonmusk`). Either `userName` or `userId` is required.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_followers_ids#parameter-user-id)

userId

string<string>

Numeric user ID. Either `userName` or `userId` is required.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_followers_ids#parameter-count)

count

integer

default:5000

Number of follower IDs to return per page. Min 50, max 5000. Default 5000 (bulk pull, recommended).

Required range: `50 <= x <= 5000`

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_followers_ids#parameter-cursor)

cursor

string<string>

Pagination cursor — pass the `next_cursor` value from the previous response. First page: omit or pass empty string.

#### Response

200

application/json

Follower IDs

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_followers_ids#response-ids)

ids

string\[\]

Array of follower IDs.

Numeric Twitter user ID, returned as string to preserve precision (Twitter IDs exceed JS Number safe range).

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_followers_ids#response-has-next-page)

has\_next\_page

boolean

True if more pages are available (next\_cursor is non-empty and not '0').

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_followers_ids#response-next-cursor)

next\_cursor

string

Cursor for the next page. Empty string or '0' means no more pages.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_followers_ids#response-status)

status

enum<string>

Status of the request — 'success' or 'error'.

Available options:

`success`,

`error`

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_followers_ids#response-msg)

msg

string

Status message.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_followers_ids#response-code)

code

integer

Status code (0 = success).

X-API-Key

string

header

required

#### Query Parameters

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/list_timeline#parameter-list-id)

listId

string<string>

required

cursor

string<string>

The cursor to paginate through the results. First page is "".

tweets

object\[\]

required

has\_next\_page

boolean

required

Indicates if there are more results available

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/list_timeline#response-next-cursor)

next\_cursor

string

required

Cursor for fetching the next page of results

cURL

cURL

    curl --request GET \
      --url https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/user/tweet_timeline \
      --header 'X-API-Key: <api-key>'

    import requests
    
    url = "https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/user/tweet_timeline"
    
    headers = {"X-API-Key": "<api-key>"}
    
    response = requests.get(url, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {method: 'GET', headers: {'X-API-Key': '<api-key>'}};
    
    fetch('https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/user/tweet_timeline', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    <?php
    
    $curl = curl_init();
    
    curl_setopt_array($curl, [\
      CURLOPT_URL => "https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/user/tweet_timeline",\
      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "GET",\
      CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => [\
        "X-API-Key: <api-key>"\
      ],\
    ]);
    
    $response = curl_exec($curl);
    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);
    
    if ($err) {
      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
    } else {
      echo $response;
    }

    package main
    
    import (
    	"fmt"
    	"net/http"
    	"io"
    )
    
    func main() {
    
    	url := "https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/user/tweet_timeline"
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("GET", url, nil)
    
    	req.Header.Add("X-API-Key", "<api-key>")
    
    	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
    
    	defer res.Body.Close()
    	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
    
    	fmt.Println(string(body))
    
    }

    HttpResponse<String> response = Unirest.get("https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/user/tweet_timeline")
      .header("X-API-Key", "<api-key>")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'
    require 'net/http'
    
    url = URI("https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/user/tweet_timeline")
    
    http = Net::HTTP.new(url.host, url.port)
    http.use_ssl = true
    
    request = Net::HTTP::Get.new(url)
    request["X-API-Key"] = '<api-key>'
    
    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

#### Authorizations

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_timeline#authorization-x-api-key)

X-API-Key

string

header

required

#### Query Parameters

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_timeline#parameter-user-id)

userId

string<string>

user id of the user.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_timeline#parameter-include-replies)

includeReplies

boolean

default:false

Whether to include replies in the results. Default is False.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_timeline#parameter-include-parent-tweet)

includeParentTweet

boolean

default:false

Whether to include the parent tweet when the tweet is a reply. Default is False.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_timeline#parameter-cursor)

cursor

string<string>

The cursor to paginate through the results. First page is "".

#### Response

200

application/json

Tweets response

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_timeline#response-tweets)

tweets

object\[\]

required

Array of tweets

Show child attributes

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_timeline#response-has-next-page)

has\_next\_page

boolean

required

Indicates if there are more results available

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_timeline#response-next-cursor)

next\_cursor

string

required

Cursor for fetching the next page of results

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_timeline#response-status)

status

enum<string>

required

Status of the request.success or error

Available options:

`success`,

`error`

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_timeline#response-message)

message

string

required

Message of the request.error message

string

header

required

#### Query Parameters

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_replies_v2#parameter-tweet-id)

tweetId

string<string>

required

The tweet ID to get replies for. eg. 1846987139428634858

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_replies_v2#parameter-cursor)

cursor

string<string>

The cursor to paginate through the results. First page is "".

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_replies_v2#parameter-query-type)

queryType

enum<string>

default:Relevance

Sort order for replies. Default is Relevance.

Available options:

`Relevance`,

`Latest`,

`Likes`

200

application/json

Tweet replies response

tweets

has\_next\_page

boolean

next\_cursor

string

status

enum<string>

#### Authorizations

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_thread_context#authorization-x-api-key)

X-API-Key

string

header

required

#### Query Parameters

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_thread_context#parameter-tweet-id)

tweetId

string<string>

required

The tweet ID to get. Can be a reply tweet or a original tweet.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_thread_context#parameter-cursor)

cursor

string<string>

The cursor to paginate through the results. First page is "".

#### Response

200

application/json

Tweet replies response

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_thread_context#response-replies)

replies

object\[\]

Array of tweets

Show child attributes

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_thread_context#response-has-next-page)

has\_next\_page

boolean

Indicates if there are more results available. If true, use next\_cursor to fetch the next page. Warning: Due to Twitter API inconsistency, has\_more might return true even when no additional data exists. In such cases, subsequent requests will return empty results - this is a known platform limitation.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_thread_context#response-next-cursor)

next\_cursor

string

Cursor for fetching the next page of results

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_thread_context#response-status)

status

enum<string>

Status of the request.success or error

Available options:

`success`,

`error`

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_thread_context#response-message)

message

string

Message of the request.error message

#### Authorizations

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_mention#authorization-x-api-key)

X-API-Key

string

header

required

#### Query Parameters

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_mention#parameter-user-name)

userName

string<string>

required

The user screen name to get. eg. kaitoEasyApi

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_mention#parameter-since-time)

sinceTime

integer<int64>

On or after a specified unix timestamp in seconds.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_mention#parameter-until-time)

untilTime

integer<int64>

Before a specified unix timestamp in seconds.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_mention#parameter-cursor)

cursor

string<string>

The cursor to paginate through the results. First page is "".

#### Response

200

application/json

Tweet quotes response

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_mention#response-tweets)

tweets

object\[\]

Array of tweets

Show child attributes

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_mention#response-has-next-page)

has\_next\_page

boolean

Indicates if there are more results available. If true, use next\_cursor to fetch the next page. Warning: Due to Twitter API inconsistency, has\_more might return true even when no additional data exists. In such cases, subsequent requests will return empty results - this is a known platform limitation.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_mention#response-next-cursor)

next\_cursor

string

Cursor for fetching the next page of results

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_mention#response-status)

status

enum<string>

Status of the request.success or error

Available options:

`success`,

`error`

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_user_mention#response-message)

message

string

Message of the request.error message

#### Authorizations

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/tweet_advanced_search#authorization-x-api-key)

X-API-Key

string

header

required

#### Query Parameters

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/tweet_advanced_search#parameter-query)

query

string<string>

required

The query to search for.eg. "AI" OR "Twitter" from:elonmusk since\_time:1776045662 until\_time:1776081762 .please don't use since:2021-12-31\_23:59:59\_UTC and until:2021-12-31\_23:59:59\_UTC, it's not supported now!!! Get more examples from [https://github.com/igorbrigadir/twitter-advanced-search](https://github.com/igorbrigadir/twitter-advanced-search)

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/tweet_advanced_search#parameter-query-type)

queryType

enum<string>

default:Latest

required

The query type to search for.eg. "Latest" OR "Top"

Available options:

`Latest`,

`Top`

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/tweet_advanced_search#parameter-cursor)

cursor

string<string>

The cursor to paginate through the results. First page is "".

#### Response

200

cURL

cURL

    curl --request GET \
      --url https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/tweet/quotes \
      --header 'X-API-Key: <api-key>'

    import requests
    
    url = "https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/tweet/quotes"
    
    headers = {"X-API-Key": "<api-key>"}
    
    response = requests.get(url, headers=headers)
    
    print(response.text)

    const options = {method: 'GET', headers: {'X-API-Key': '<api-key>'}};
    
    fetch('https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/tweet/quotes', options)
      .then(res => res.json())
      .then(res => console.log(res))
      .catch(err => console.error(err));

    <?php
    
    $curl = curl_init();
    
    curl_setopt_array($curl, [\
      CURLOPT_URL => "https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/tweet/quotes",\
      CURLOPT_RETURNTRANSFER => true,\
      CURLOPT_ENCODING => "",\
      CURLOPT_MAXREDIRS => 10,\
      CURLOPT_TIMEOUT => 30,\
      CURLOPT_HTTP_VERSION => CURL_HTTP_VERSION_1_1,\
      CURLOPT_CUSTOMREQUEST => "GET",\
      CURLOPT_HTTPHEADER => [\
        "X-API-Key: <api-key>"\
      ],\
    ]);
    
    $response = curl_exec($curl);
    $err = curl_error($curl);
    
    curl_close($curl);
    
    if ($err) {
      echo "cURL Error #:" . $err;
    } else {
      echo $response;
    }

    package main
    
    import (
    	"fmt"
    	"net/http"
    	"io"
    )
    
    func main() {
    
    	url := "https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/tweet/quotes"
    
    	req, _ := http.NewRequest("GET", url, nil)
    
    	req.Header.Add("X-API-Key", "<api-key>")
    
    	res, _ := http.DefaultClient.Do(req)
    
    	defer res.Body.Close()
    	body, _ := io.ReadAll(res.Body)
    
    	fmt.Println(string(body))
    
    }

    HttpResponse<String> response = Unirest.get("https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/tweet/quotes")
      .header("X-API-Key", "<api-key>")
      .asString();

    require 'uri'
    require 'net/http'
    
    url = URI("https://api.twitterapi.io/twitter/tweet/quotes")
    
    http = Net::HTTP.new(url.host, url.port)
    http.use_ssl = true
    
    request = Net::HTTP::Get.new(url)
    request["X-API-Key"] = '<api-key>'
    
    response = http.request(request)
    puts response.read_body

#### Authorizations

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_quote#authorization-x-api-key)

X-API-Key

string

header

required

#### Query Parameters

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_quote#parameter-tweet-id)

tweetId

string<string>

required

The tweet ID to get. eg. 1846987139428634858

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_quote#parameter-since-time)

sinceTime

integer<int64>

On or after a specified unix timestamp in seconds.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_quote#parameter-until-time)

untilTime

integer<int64>

Before a specified unix timestamp in seconds.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_quote#parameter-include-replies)

includeReplies

boolean

default:true

Whether to include replies in the results. Default is True.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_quote#parameter-cursor)

cursor

string<string>

The cursor to paginate through the results. First page is "".

#### Response

200

application/json

Tweet quotes response

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_quote#response-tweets)

tweets

object\[\]

Array of tweets

Show child attributes

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_quote#response-has-next-page)

has\_next\_page

boolean

Indicates if there are more results available. If true, use next\_cursor to fetch the next page. Warning: Due to Twitter API inconsistency, has\_more might return true even when no additional data exists. In such cases, subsequent requests will return empty results - this is a known platform limitation.

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_quote#response-next-cursor)

next\_cursor

string

Cursor for fetching the next page of results

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_quote#response-status)

status

enum<string>

Status of the request.success or error

Available options:

`success`,

`error`

[​](https://docs.twitterapi.io/api-reference/endpoint/get_tweet_quote#response-message)

message

string

Message of the request.error message
